### 0.1.19 Claude Code hook diagnostic events — source and implementation checkpoints

The pinned-source comparison is against DeepSeek Harness commit [`5badb15009ae1756c3afe0ae0cef1faafc290ccc`](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/tree/5badb15009ae1756c3afe0ae0cef1faafc290ccc). The initial native runtime candidate is frozen at [`6b2b9ec`](https://github.com/f4ah6o/dsh.mbt/tree/6b2b9ec887e01e58761d1f5daf7d63536d5045be); signal-exit correction [`9413f6a`](https://github.com/f4ah6o/dsh.mbt/commit/9413f6a180dd88639405ea9c1819c8dec3038a9e) is included in final candidate [`3cc969e`](https://github.com/f4ah6o/dsh.mbt/tree/3cc969eddf77d4334b30d147ccdfee1882c61a7d). Source observations, executed Engine findings, adapter decisions, and implementation-agent test results are separated below.

Sol's independent whole-feature review of exact candidate [`627c569`](https://github.com/f4ah6o/dsh.mbt/tree/627c569a5cd81ae50f09f0e0f11ae00cd769e964) against clean main `518254e5b0ebe81966958f85f247828de866fe41` is **APPROVE** (3 passes / all 7 viewpoints / zero unresolved). DEBT-040–043 are independently verified fixed. Sol's five-case runtime matrix passes 5 / 5, and the full exact-source runtime suite passes 187 / 187 (181 repository tests plus 6 private reviewer cases, including those same five). The final review also confirms the initial Engine/API foundation's 52 / 52 matrix and 568 / 568 package suite. Luna's final `3cc969e` native worker passed runtime 181 / 181 and ACP stdio; Luna's clean `627c569` build and check pass. Root's final package preview validates all 27 changed files. Hosted CI and platform builds for PR #53 remain in progress; merge and publication are pending.

- **Pinned event contract**: [`events.ts`](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/5badb15009ae1756c3afe0ae0cef1faafc290ccc/packages/hooks/hook-protocol/src/events.ts#L1-L5) defines `hook/invoked` and `hook/result` as log-only events with no surface intent; both must be paired inside an open turn. The Claude Code bridge appends them only when both a session and turn are available: the invocation record is appended before `runHook`, and the result record afterward ([`index.ts`](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/5badb15009ae1756c3afe0ae0cef1faafc290ccc/packages/hooks/hooks-claude-code/src/index.ts#L148-L188)). `Session.append` adds accepted events synchronously to its in-memory log and notifies observers; persistence plugins buffer asynchronously, so this ordering alone does not establish completed storage I/O ([`core/session/src/index.ts`](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/5badb15009ae1756c3afe0ae0cef1faafc290ccc/packages/core/session/src/index.ts#L682-L687)). UserPromptSubmit, PreToolUse, PostToolUse, and Stop pass a turn. SessionStart and SubagentStart/SubagentStop do not, so those lifecycle points do not emit this pair ([`index.ts`](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/5badb15009ae1756c3afe0ae0cef1faafc290ccc/packages/hooks/hooks-claude-code/src/index.ts#L196-L283)).
- **Per-command result fields**: `hook/invoked` carries `turn`, string `point`, dialect, stable `handlerId`, and an optional matcher. `hook/result` correlates on `handlerId` and carries the point, parsed decision, optional numeric exit code, optional bounded stderr summary, and measured wall-clock duration ([`events.ts`](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/5badb15009ae1756c3afe0ae0cef1faafc290ccc/packages/hooks/hook-protocol/src/events.ts#L11-L42), [`types.ts`](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/5badb15009ae1756c3afe0ae0cef1faafc290ccc/packages/hooks/hook-protocol/src/types.ts#L6-L45)). The result decision is `output.decision`, then `stop` when `continue === false`, otherwise `pass`. The bridge records each handler result before merging; it is not the final merged hook-point decision. The latter separately follows deny > ask > allow precedence, keeps the first stop reason, and accumulates context/messages in hook order ([`events.ts`](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/5badb15009ae1756c3afe0ae0cef1faafc290ccc/packages/hooks/hook-protocol/src/events.ts#L79-L96), [`merge.ts`](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/5badb15009ae1756c3afe0ae0cef1faafc290ccc/packages/hooks/hook-protocol/src/merge.ts#L10-L29)).
- **Summary and timing**: a missing process exit remains absent; the runner returns a measured duration from its supplied clock, including for infrastructure failures represented as an outcome ([`runner.ts`](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/5badb15009ae1756c3afe0ae0cef1faafc290ccc/packages/hooks/hook-protocol/src/runner.ts#L45-L50), [`runner.ts`](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/5badb15009ae1756c3afe0ae0cef1faafc290ccc/packages/hooks/hook-protocol/src/runner.ts#L62-L98)). The bridge trims stderr and uses a configurable UTF-16-code-unit prefix limit (500 by default); it appends an ellipsis when more text remains beyond that prefix ([`events.ts`](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/5badb15009ae1756c3afe0ae0cef1faafc290ccc/packages/hooks/hook-protocol/src/events.ts#L49-L62), [`events.ts`](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/5badb15009ae1756c3afe0ae0cef1faafc290ccc/packages/hooks/hook-protocol/src/events.ts#L79-L96)).
- **Retained MoonBit seams and native scope**: the implementation keeps the generic Json event-data boundary (**DEBT-001**), String error results (**DEBT-003**), and manually copied Engine state (**DEBT-004**). Root PreToolUse, PostToolUse, Stop, and UserPromptSubmit continuations inside an existing turn write durable pairs to the root session. Noninitial child UserPromptSubmit, tool, and Stop hooks write transient pairs to that child's ephemeral Engine; records are not relocated to the parent. SessionStart, SubagentStart, and SubagentStop lifecycle commands do not emit diagnostic pairs, matching the pinned bridge's lack of a turn at those points. Each direct root `session_send` UserPromptSubmit runs before a new turn exists, and the initial foreground-child UserPromptSubmit also runs while its child Engine is idle; both pre-turn pairs remain absent under the existing admission boundary. This is a native scope gap in DEBT-039, not an upstream omission. The implementation does not redesign prompt sequencing. The read-only v4 importer is outside this parity checkpoint unless review expands that scope with evidence.
- **Pairing across interruption**: the candidate checkpoints a root invocation before starting its command; child invocation records stay in the child Engine. A completed infrastructure failure records its decoded failure summary, pass decision, omitted exit code, and measured duration. If cancellation or recovery leaves an invocation without a result, it remains unresolved; the runtime does not invent a result or pair it across turns. Open-turn EOF may retain a pending invocation, and cancelled/interrupted turns may remain unresolved; normal completed, blocked, provider-error, or max-steps closure requires all pairs settled. Pending state is cleared when a new turn starts. Engine settlement invariants and the whole feature have independent approval at `627c569`; hosted CI, merge, and release remain pending.

#### DEBT-038: hook diagnostic contract has shared string-valued fields

- Origin / kind / priority / state: `UPSTREAM, MOONBIT / design, idiom`, priority unassessed, `observed`. This is a type-shape observation, not a confirmed defect.
- Upstream baseline / path: [`events.ts`](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/5badb15009ae1756c3afe0ae0cef1faafc290ccc/packages/hooks/hook-protocol/src/events.ts#L11-L42) and [`types.ts`](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/5badb15009ae1756c3afe0ae0cef1faafc290ccc/packages/hooks/hook-protocol/src/types.ts#L6-L45). `point` is a string, `handlerId` is a string, the result `decision` is a string, and the dialect is a finite `HookDialect` union. The event map declares the two payload shapes, but it does not define a hook-point-specific union tying a point to its valid payload. Invocation and result are separate records correlated by `handlerId`.
- Port baseline / path: frozen candidate [`6b2b9ec`](https://github.com/f4ah6o/dsh.mbt/blob/6b2b9ec887e01e58761d1f5daf7d63536d5045be/runtime/hooks_runtime.mbt) and [`engine/types.mbt`](https://github.com/f4ah6o/dsh.mbt/blob/6b2b9ec887e01e58761d1f5daf7d63536d5045be/engine/types.mbt). Native diagnostic fields remain string-valued, and persisted event payloads use the existing generic Json seam.
- Observation / concern: this implementation retains the existing Json payload and String error boundaries and manually copies Engine state. Handler IDs increment in the `NativeHooks` instance initialized when its config loads and shared with child execution; the pinned bridge uses a process-global counter. This is an identity-scope design difference, not a reproduced collision or runtime defect. The diagnostic decision remains separate from the adapter's operational action. Duration uses elapsed monotonic `@async.now()` time. Stderr uses JavaScript-compatible trimming and a default 500-UTF-16-unit prefix cap plus an ellipsis. MoonBit cannot represent an isolated surrogate, so if the boundary splits an astral scalar the adapter keeps the whole-scalar prefix (up to 499 units) before the ellipsis; this is an explicit adapter difference from the source's possible lone high surrogate, not a reproduced upstream bug.
- Follow-up / acceptance: any future typed internal point/decision model must preserve source-compatible wire strings, unknown-event handling, and replay. The read-only v4 importer remains out of this increment. These source and adapter observations require no type redesign and do not establish a runtime defect.

#### DEBT-039: pre-turn UserPromptSubmit runs have no native turn envelope

- Origin / kind / priority / state: `PORT, CROSSCUT / compatibility, design`, priority unassessed, `observed`. This is an acknowledged adapter-scope gap, not an upstream omission or a reproduced runtime bug.
- Upstream baseline / path: [`hooks-claude-code/src/index.ts`](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/5badb15009ae1756c3afe0ae0cef1faafc290ccc/packages/hooks/hooks-claude-code/src/index.ts#L211-L215) passes the open turn to UserPromptSubmit, and `runPoint` records diagnostics when a session and turn are present ([`index.ts`](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/5badb15009ae1756c3afe0ae0cef1faafc290ccc/packages/hooks/hooks-claude-code/src/index.ts#L148-L180)).
- Port baseline / path: frozen candidate [`6b2b9ec`](https://github.com/f4ah6o/dsh.mbt/blob/6b2b9ec887e01e58761d1f5daf7d63536d5045be/runtime/runtime.mbt#L564-L572) routes every configured direct root `session_send` through the pre-turn hook path. That path calls `run_root_send_hooks_controlled` before the API admits the new turn, without a diagnostic sink ([`runtime.mbt`](https://github.com/f4ah6o/dsh.mbt/blob/6b2b9ec887e01e58761d1f5daf7d63536d5045be/runtime/runtime.mbt#L853-L875), [`runtime.mbt`](https://github.com/f4ah6o/dsh.mbt/blob/6b2b9ec887e01e58761d1f5daf7d63536d5045be/runtime/runtime.mbt#L908-L985)).
- Observation / impact: this applies to each new root turn admitted through direct `session_send`, not just the first prompt in a session. The initial foreground-child hook likewise starts while the child Engine is idle; `begin_subagent_prompt_hook` stages the input and only `accept_subagent_prompt_hook` appends `turn/start` ([`engine/subagent_hooks.mbt`](https://github.com/f4ah6o/dsh.mbt/blob/6b2b9ec887e01e58761d1f5daf7d63536d5045be/engine/subagent_hooks.mbt#L81-L119), [`engine/subagent_hooks.mbt`](https://github.com/f4ah6o/dsh.mbt/blob/6b2b9ec887e01e58761d1f5daf7d63536d5045be/engine/subagent_hooks.mbt#L181-L224)); runtime passes no diagnostic sink for that initial child prompt ([`subagents.mbt`](https://github.com/f4ah6o/dsh.mbt/blob/6b2b9ec887e01e58761d1f5daf7d63536d5045be/runtime/subagents.mbt#L640-L647)). Mid-turn root UserPromptSubmit reached from PostTool and Stop continuation remains traced. The missing pairs are native scope gaps, not omissions in the upstream bridge.
- Compatibility decision / follow-up: keep current root prompt sequencing and child-local attribution. Do not fabricate a turn or move child records into the parent. Revisit only with a separate boundary design that does not replay the command or misstate its turn.
- Completion evidence: source review establishes these boundaries; Sol's whole-feature review at `627c569` approves the implementation, and Luna's `3cc969e` fresh-worker runtime suite passed 181 / 181 with ACP stdio smoke. Hosted CI, merge, and release remain pending.

The initial Engine/API findings came from independent probes of candidate 847ef78d57b7463e4b6d709448a16eee005d9625; they were port defects, not upstream findings. The failed 847 matrix, partial fixes at f2f7bda and ea4f906, and their review dispositions remain recorded in the history below. Final correction [`165a13b`](https://github.com/f4ah6o/dsh.mbt/tree/165a13bef3ed496bb5754d2fec8a41f2ae9b19b4) closes DEBT-040, DEBT-041, and DEBT-042 at the Engine/API layer; it does not constitute whole-feature runtime approval.

#### DEBT-040: maximum hook-result reservation omits the terminal-closure projection

- Origin / kind / priority / state: PORT / bug, correctness, priority high (a valid result could be rejected at the session capacity boundary), fixed and independently approved at the Engine/API layer. Whole-feature review and release remain pending. This was a MoonBit candidate defect, not an upstream issue.
- Upstream baseline / path: N/A. The reservation and closure mismatch is in the port's Engine capacity admission.
- Port baseline / path: candidate 847ef78d57b7463e4b6d709448a16eee005d9625, engine/hook_events.mbt:93-110 projects a maximum pending result, and engine/transaction.mbt:166-227 checks that projection but then checks the interrupted terminal closure from the unprojected starting state.
- Observation / reproducer / result: Sol's exact-candidate probe at /Volumes/devstorage/Developer/dsh-hook-diagnostics-review-20261012/reviewer_hook_foundation_wbtest.mbt:59-95 found an admitted invocation with live serialized length 258,919 and projected result length 262,143. A valid result write using the maximum positive exit code and duration plus a 501-unit summary returned an error, although the projected result fits the 262,144-unit session cap. The probe and four-backend matrix log are /Volumes/devstorage/Developer/dsh-hook-diagnostics-review-20261012/reviewer_hook_foundation_wbtest.mbt (SHA-256 016f22da0be57102b381645cf40f0273e846c48071f4d33acbe11862f018c3a7) and /Volumes/devstorage/Developer/dsh-hook-diagnostics-review-20261012/foundation-all-probes.log. An extended 847ef78 probe then tested a minimum negative exit with a 501-NUL summary and three pending results settled in reverse order; both cases failed on all four backends (0 / 8) in /Volumes/devstorage/Developer/dsh-hook-diagnostics-review-20261012/foundation-cap-extended.log. Its preserved source is /Volumes/devstorage/Developer/dsh-hook-diagnostics-review-20261012/reviewer_hook_foundation_extended_wbtest.mbt (SHA-256 d544a2f2f6b6d0ed1bb21bfa9531eefb9601d39393bb350ed6b12078e8f6a17b).
- Additional boundary observation: on candidate 847ef78d57b7463e4b6d709448a16eee005d9625, the writer accepted signed Int exit codes but its reservation used 2147483647 (10 decimal digits) while -2147483648 needs 11. This is a source-based size calculation, not a separately executed probe, and has the same one-unit-per-pending-result under-reservation risk.
- Impact / follow-up: near the cap, a valid hook result could be refused despite invocation admission having reserved for its maximum result and interruption closure. The correction projects pending results together with terminal closure and reserves for the longest accepted signed integer.
- Completion evidence: `f2f7bda` was an intermediate correction; final Engine/API head `165a13b` passes Sol's 13-case matrix on four backends (52 / 52) and the full Engine suite on each backend (142 / 142; 568 total including those same 13 probes). Sol's final foundation review is APPROVE, 3 passes / 7 viewpoints / zero unresolved. Immutable source: `/Volumes/devstorage/Developer/dsh-hook-diagnostics-review-20261012/reviewer_hook_foundation_165a13b_wbtest.mbt` (SHA-256 `0d6be5ade906e29757682d19e8a021d2dc3ff89710cc9954b2943b2f677980b1`); report: `/Volumes/devstorage/Developer/dsh-hook-diagnostics-review-20261012/foundation-review-165a13b.md` (SHA-256 `502a0d2afe27b072c8375ca2577a7dd9ca3915dae28ab744f5b6211c6b10e1c0`). Whole-feature review is approved at `627c569`; hosted CI, merge, and release remain pending.

#### DEBT-041: normal closure or PostTool advancement can bypass pending hook results

- Origin / kind / priority / state: PORT / bug, correctness, priority high (normal advancement could close or dispatch before a hook result), fixed and independently approved at the Engine/API layer. This was a MoonBit candidate defect, not an upstream issue.
- Upstream baseline / path: N/A. This is a port Engine settlement and advancement invariant, not an upstream bug claim.
- Port baseline / path: candidate 847ef78d57b7463e4b6d709448a16eee005d9625: a pending-invocation guard covered LLM completion, but block_stop_continuation, block_post_tool_prompt_hook, and last-tool max_steps closure could still end the turn unresolved. The reader rejects terminal history with an unresolved invocation at engine/persistence.mbt:1968. Correction f2f7bda605c6ccd72f4e54fd5c0441ebcbdab441 guards those reproduced closures. The corrected audit then found Engine::accept_post_tool_prompt_hook could still advance with a pending UserPromptSubmit result; correction ea4f906d18cb7f1f7194afdc5cc77bebd95867f0 adds a pre-mutation guard and regression.
- Observation / reproducer / result: on 847ef78, probes recorded unresolved UserPromptSubmit or PreToolUse invocations and called block_stop_continuation, block_post_tool_prompt_hook, or completed the final tool at max_steps=1. Each operation returned Ok while restoring its terminal snapshot returned Err(Turn ended before native hook results were recorded); these paths reproduced on all four backends. The f2 audit then found pending-result advancement at accept_post_tool_prompt_hook (0 / 4), and the ea4 audit found analogous child prompt accept/block paths (0 / 8). The final `165a13b` correction guards root and child accept/block paths before mutation and allows progress after result settlement. Sol's immutable 13-case matrix passes the corrected root and child variants on each backend (52 / 52 overall). Evidence: `/Volumes/devstorage/Developer/dsh-hook-diagnostics-review-20261012/foundation-review-165a13b.md`, `/Volumes/devstorage/Developer/dsh-hook-diagnostics-review-20261012/child-guard-fixed-probes.log`, and the preserved source listed under DEBT-040.
- Static-only path: engine/stop_hooks.mbt:271-284 also terminates continue_stop_hook on max_steps or effect-ID exhaustion. That path has not been executed with a pending diagnostic invocation and is not counted among the reproduced cases.
- Impact / follow-up: normal turn advancement waits for pending hook results before consuming a claimed PostTool boundary, closing root or child turns, or dispatching the next provider effect. Cancelled/interrupted turns may retain unresolved invocations without fabricated results; pending calls do not cross turns. The max-step/effect-limit `continue_stop_hook` path was static-only on the failed candidate; the final Engine suite and independent matrix approve the implemented settlement contract.
- Completion evidence: final correction `165a13b` adds root and child pre-mutation guards and regressions that require rejection to preserve snapshots and emit no effects, then permit progress after result settlement. Sol's exact-head 13-case matrix passes on all four backends (52 / 52); the full Engine suite passes 142 / 142 per backend (568 total, including the matrix cases). Final review: `/Volumes/devstorage/Developer/dsh-hook-diagnostics-review-20261012/foundation-review-165a13b.md` (SHA-256 `502a0d2afe27b072c8375ca2577a7dd9ca3915dae28ab744f5b6211c6b10e1c0`). Whole-feature review is approved at `627c569`; hosted CI, merge, and release remain pending.

#### DEBT-042: invocation writer accepts a matcher its snapshot reader rejects

- Origin / kind / priority / state: PORT / bug, consistency, priority low (writer-created history could be non-restorable), fixed and independently approved at the Engine/API layer. This was a MoonBit candidate defect, not an upstream issue.
- Upstream baseline / path: N/A. The mismatch is between the native invocation writer and its snapshot reader.
- Port baseline / path: candidate 847ef78d57b7463e4b6d709448a16eee005d9625, engine/hook_events.mbt:140-150 checks matcher length but accepts embedded NUL; engine/persistence.mbt:1718-1723 rejects a matcher containing NUL.
- Observation / reproducer / result: Sol's preserved probe source /Volumes/devstorage/Developer/dsh-hook-diagnostics-review-20261012/reviewer_hook_foundation_wbtest.mbt:2-10 writes matcher r\u0000ead successfully, then restoring the Engine's own snapshot returns Err(Invalid native hook matcher). The case reproduced on all four backends in /Volumes/devstorage/Developer/dsh-hook-diagnostics-review-20261012/foundation-all-probes.log; source SHA is recorded under DEBT-040. Ordinary hook configuration already filters this matcher, so the confirmed mismatch is at the public Engine/API boundary. The correction rejects embedded NUL before mutation, and the fixed-head matrix verifies both unchanged-state rejection and own-snapshot restore.
- Follow-up / acceptance: align writer admission and restore validation so every accepted invocation snapshot restores successfully; retain the unchanged-state rejection regression for embedded NUL and preserve the existing length boundary.
- Completion evidence: final correction `165a13b` rejects embedded NUL before mutation. Sol's approved 13-case matrix passes on all four backends (52 / 52), and the full Engine package suite passes 142 / 142 per backend. The immutable matrix source and final report are listed under DEBT-040. Whole-feature review is approved at `627c569`; hosted CI, merge, and release remain pending.

#### DEBT-043: signal termination is recorded as a numeric exit code

- Origin / kind / priority / state: PORT / correctness, priority medium, `fixed` and independently verified at the final feature candidate. This was a reproduced runtime serialization mismatch, not an upstream bug claim.
- Upstream baseline / path: pinned runner results distinguish an optional exit code from other termination outcomes ([`runner.ts`](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/5badb15009ae1756c3afe0ae0cef1faafc290ccc/packages/hooks/hook-protocol/src/runner.ts#L62-L98)); a process killed by signal has no numeric process exit code for `hook/result`.
- Port baseline / path: frozen candidate `6b2b9ec887e01e58761d1f5daf7d63536d5045be`, `runtime/hooks_process.mbt` and `runtime/hooks_runtime.mbt`, passed the negative `process.wait()` status through as `Some(exit_code)`.
- Observation / reproducer / result: Sol's fresh-worker probe ran a `SIGKILL` handler and a separate handler exiting 143. Candidate 6b wrote `Some(-9)` for the signal death and correctly kept `Some(143)` for the genuine numeric exit; three independent cases produced 2 passes / 1 failure. The failed fixture uses KILL; an earlier TERM-only fixture was invalid because that process ignored the signal. Immutable source `/Volumes/devstorage/Developer/dsh-hook-diagnostics-review-20261012/reviewer_hook_runtime_6b2b9ec_signal_wbtest.mbt` (SHA-256 `b27b50c4452a3f2f7b992ed108fb0c5ddc306a962cbf2bc4f9e7c4ebee4088f8`); log `/Volumes/devstorage/Developer/dsh-hook-diagnostics-review-20261012/runtime-signal-confirmed-probes.log` (SHA-256 `473e868399f72ad8e2768500d74e1c34ca793393b954a0afc4e4a063829ffda4`).
- Fix / independent verification: correction `9413f6a180dd88639405ea9c1819c8dec3038a9e` omits negative completed-process status while retaining genuine nonnegative exits. Sol's expanded five-case matrix on the fresh `3cc969e` worker passes 5 / 5, including omission for KILL and retention of exit 143; the immutable six-case probe source is `/Volumes/devstorage/Developer/dsh-hook-diagnostics-review-20261012/reviewer_hook_runtime_3cc969e_expanded6_wbtest.mbt` (SHA-256 `627474e436c2c6fc508f1d62408fdfb81f195b0aab779f83455b43af413310fe`). Sol's full runtime package run passes 187 / 187 (181 repository tests + 6 private reviewer tests, including the five-case matrix); do not add matrix counts to the full-suite count. Worker SHA-256 `2adb47627dd53f27339f6ba6cf92fd0993509702929f659aef6d8d9cb8d65c39`; full log `/Volumes/devstorage/Developer/dsh-hook-diagnostics-review-20261012/runtime-final-full-3cc969e.log` (SHA-256 `04cbcfe7331d2e3f4c004586f9d92506414184833e8757ae63dc053e0f806f14`). Sol's exact whole-feature review of `627c569` against `518254e` is APPROVE (3 passes / 7 viewpoints / zero unresolved); report `/Volumes/devstorage/Developer/dsh-hook-diagnostics-review-20261012/feature-review-627c569.md` (SHA-256 `52822aa3d469714745673bba5cfb356bca0822fd0bfe7cc5e85818ca1c3f7ed9`). All-package native check: 140 tasks, 0 errors, 5 warnings (4 existing vendor warnings and 1 private fixture debug warning), with no production-owned warning introduced. This is independent reviewer evidence, separate from Luna's 181 / 181 implementation run. Hosted CI, merge, and release remain pending.

### Next hook slice: Codex five-event command hooks — pinned-source checkpoint

The source baseline remains DeepSeek Harness [5badb15009ae1756c3afe0ae0cef1faafc290ccc](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/tree/5badb15009ae1756c3afe0ae0cef1faafc290ccc). This is a static source contract for the next bridge slice, based on [hooks-codex/src/index.ts](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/5badb15009ae1756c3afe0ae0cef1faafc290ccc/packages/hooks/hooks-codex/src/index.ts), [hooks-codex/src/config.ts](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/5badb15009ae1756c3afe0ae0cef1faafc290ccc/packages/hooks/hooks-codex/src/config.ts), and [hook-protocol/src](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/tree/5badb15009ae1756c3afe0ae0cef1faafc290ccc/packages/hooks/hook-protocol/src). It records source semantics, not a native parity claim or a reproduced upstream runtime defect.

- **Events and config intake:** the parser recognizes SessionStart, UserPromptSubmit, PreToolUse, PostToolUse, and Stop only. It accepts a bare event map or a { hooks: … } wrapper; unknown events and malformed groups or hook entries are skipped. A missing type defaults to command; unsupported hook types and async: true are skipped and warned, and only synchronous commands are retained. An invalid matcher on a runnable matcher-bearing event rejects the parsed config; apply catches that failure and registers no hooks. UserPromptSubmit and Stop discard matcher fields before validation. One process-level configPath is read during plugin application. Per-hook numeric timeout takes precedence over numeric timeoutSec; either is stored as seconds and the protocol runner multiplies it by 1,000. The bridge default is 600,000 ms (600 seconds). Source references: [config.ts](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/5badb15009ae1756c3afe0ae0cef1faafc290ccc/packages/hooks/hooks-codex/src/config.ts#L10-L85), [index.ts](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/5badb15009ae1756c3afe0ae0cef1faafc290ccc/packages/hooks/hooks-codex/src/index.ts#L49-L71) and [index.ts](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/5badb15009ae1756c3afe0ae0cef1faafc290ccc/packages/hooks/hooks-codex/src/index.ts#L87-L110), [runner.ts](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/5badb15009ae1756c3afe0ae0cef1faafc290ccc/packages/hooks/hook-protocol/src/runner.ts#L67-L84).
- **Matchers and order:** PreToolUse and PostToolUse match on the tool name; SessionStart matches on its source. UserPromptSubmit and Stop discard configured matcher fields. For Codex, absent, empty, and the literal star matcher select all; every other accepted matcher is an unanchored regular expression. Matched groups and their commands execute serially in configuration order, with each command awaited before the next. Source references: [config.ts](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/5badb15009ae1756c3afe0ae0cef1faafc290ccc/packages/hooks/hooks-codex/src/config.ts#L50-L82), [matcher.ts](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/5badb15009ae1756c3afe0ae0cef1faafc290ccc/packages/hooks/hook-protocol/src/matcher.ts#L13-L65), [index.ts](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/5badb15009ae1756c3afe0ae0cef1faafc290ccc/packages/hooks/hooks-codex/src/index.ts#L119-L155).
- **Payload defaults and shape:** the configured model defaults to the static empty string and is copied to every event; permission_mode is always "default" and transcript_path is always null. Turn-scoped events carry turn_id as a string (UserPromptSubmit stringifies its event turn; tool and Stop events stringify the latest projected turn). SessionStart has no turn ID. Tool payloads use the real tool_name but reduce every tool_input to { command }, taking a string command argument or '' otherwise; PostToolUse tool_response is flattened to text. Stop payloads always use last_assistant_message: null and stop_hook_active: false. Source references: [index.ts](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/5badb15009ae1756c3afe0ae0cef1faafc290ccc/packages/hooks/hooks-codex/src/index.ts#L204-L214), [index.ts](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/5badb15009ae1756c3afe0ae0cef1faafc290ccc/packages/hooks/hooks-codex/src/index.ts#L261-L267), [index.ts](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/5badb15009ae1756c3afe0ae0cef1faafc290ccc/packages/hooks/hooks-codex/src/index.ts#L296-L334).
- **Command boundary:** the bridge passes no hook-specific environment map and does no command substitution. It serializes payload JSON to stdin without a trailing newline. Stdout that does not start with an object opener becomes context only for SessionStart and UserPromptSubmit, and only after a clean exit when there is no structured additionalContext; nonzero output and object-shaped stdout are not used as plain context. Source references: [config.ts](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/5badb15009ae1756c3afe0ae0cef1faafc290ccc/packages/hooks/hooks-codex/src/config.ts#L1-L4), [index.ts](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/5badb15009ae1756c3afe0ae0cef1faafc290ccc/packages/hooks/hooks-codex/src/index.ts#L147-L162), [runner.ts](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/5badb15009ae1756c3afe0ae0cef1faafc290ccc/packages/hooks/hook-protocol/src/runner.ts#L22-L47) and [runner.ts](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/5badb15009ae1756c3afe0ae0cef1faafc290ccc/packages/hooks/hook-protocol/src/runner.ts#L67-L84).
- **Output controls:** the shared codec treats exit 2 as a block with stderr as its reason, and parses structured stdout only on exit 0; other nonzero exits do not block. It also parses continue: false, systemMessage, and object-valued updatedInput, but the Codex bridge does not use those fields to control the agent or rewrite a tool call. continue: false is recorded as the per-command result decision stop when no parsed permission decision takes precedence, but no event-level stop action is applied. systemMessage produces a logger warning and is not surfaced as model context. updatedInput is decoded but has no bridge consumer. additionalContext is surfaced for SessionStart, UserPromptSubmit, and PostToolUse, while PreToolUse and Stop do not consume it. Of the merged permission decisions, only deny maps to an operational action: prompt rejection, tool/result blocking, or Stop steering; allow and ask do not override the harness action. Source references: [codec.ts](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/5badb15009ae1756c3afe0ae0cef1faafc290ccc/packages/hooks/hook-protocol/src/codec.ts#L97-L133), [events.ts](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/5badb15009ae1756c3afe0ae0cef1faafc290ccc/packages/hooks/hook-protocol/src/events.ts#L85-L103), [merge.ts](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/5badb15009ae1756c3afe0ae0cef1faafc290ccc/packages/hooks/hook-protocol/src/merge.ts#L62-L99), [index.ts](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/5badb15009ae1756c3afe0ae0cef1faafc290ccc/packages/hooks/hooks-codex/src/index.ts#L204-L275).

The SessionStart lifecycle wording conflict in the pinned package README is recorded separately as DEBT-044. This was the initial source-only checkpoint; the following section records the first frozen MoonBit candidate comparison. This source audit itself did not test an implementation or reproduce an upstream runtime defect.

#### DEBT-044: Codex SessionStart lifecycle README contradicts its awaited handler

- Origin / kind / priority / state: UPSTREAM / docs, compatibility, priority unassessed, observed. This is a static documentation contradiction, not a reproduced runtime bug.
- Upstream baseline / path: the Codex handler [index.ts](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/5badb15009ae1756c3afe0ae0cef1faafc290ccc/packages/hooks/hooks-codex/src/index.ts#L191-L202) tracks its SessionStart run and then awaits it before the handler returns. The agent registry [announce](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/5badb15009ae1756c3afe0ae0cef1faafc290ccc/packages/core/agent/src/index.ts#L537-L554) awaits serial agent/created dispatch, and the loop README says queued input is released afterward ([core/agent-loop/README.md](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/5badb15009ae1756c3afe0ae0cef1faafc290ccc/packages/core/agent-loop/README.md#L108-L110)). In contrast, [hooks-codex/README.md](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/5badb15009ae1756c3afe0ae0cef1faafc290ccc/packages/hooks/hooks-codex/README.md#L81-L103) first says agent/created initialization is awaited and then says SessionStart is detached with no extension point awaiting it; its known-limitations list says the hook may miss the first request ([same README](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/5badb15009ae1756c3afe0ae0cef1faafc290ccc/packages/hooks/hooks-codex/README.md#L167-L176)). The Chinese README repeats the detached claim ([README.zh.md](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/5badb15009ae1756c3afe0ae0cef1faafc290ccc/packages/hooks/hooks-codex/README.zh.md#L81-L103), [README.zh.md](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/5badb15009ae1756c3afe0ae0cef1faafc290ccc/packages/hooks/hooks-codex/README.zh.md#L167-L176)).
- Observation / impact: the same English README describes awaited and detached behavior, while the handler and creation-dispatch source support the awaited description. This can mislead users about whether SessionStart context is available before queued input. No runtime probe was run, and this contradiction does not establish a context-loss defect.
- Follow-up / acceptance: reconcile the English and Chinese lifecycle text with the handler and dispatch contract at the pinned source. Keep a runtime timing defect separate unless a focused runtime reproducer demonstrates one.
- Fix / close status: open; no upstream documentation change or runtime fix is claimed.

### Codex five-event bridge — frozen adapter foundation checkpoint

The first frozen MoonBit candidate is [`8683b3d076a453da802d80791745b36cfd9c8aa7`](https://github.com/f4ah6o/dsh.mbt/tree/8683b3d076a453da802d80791745b36cfd9c8aa7), compared with port main [`69c51a543b40ca909db8f1f888ec5065804a75ea`](https://github.com/f4ah6o/dsh.mbt/tree/69c51a543b40ca909db8f1f888ec5065804a75ea) and the pinned upstream baseline above. Its 22-file delta adds the native Codex dialect, five event payload/decoder paths, native CLI/runtime options, and provenance fields. The feature branch had a later mutable follow-up at the time this checkpoint was written; observations here refer only to frozen `8683b3d`, not that follow-up or a final feature head.

- **Configuration and matcher boundary:** frozen `runtime/hooks_config.mbt` accepts a bare event map or `{ "hooks": ... }`, skips malformed groups and unsupported individual Codex commands, and registers only nonempty command groups for the five events. UserPromptSubmit and Stop matchers are discarded before validation, as in the pinned source. A missing or non-string command type defaults to `command`; non-command types, `async: true`, invalid/empty/NUL-containing/over-8192-character commands, and unsupported timeout values are skipped. A numeric `timeout` takes precedence over numeric `timeoutSec`; accepted values are integer seconds from 1 through 120, with a 10-second default. The pinned source default is 600 seconds. Config input is capped at 65,536 bytes. Although the shared Claude parser declares 16 groups / 32 commands per event, the Codex-specific parser does not apply those count limits. Unlike the pinned source's JavaScript regex semantics, the native parser rejects unsupported matcher syntax instead of translating it: it permits at most 128 ASCII characters, literals, ordinary groups and noncapturing `(?:...)` groups, alternation, positive ASCII character classes, quantifiers, and `^` only at the beginning / `$` only at the end, subject to MoonBit regex compilation. The supported escape set is limited to `\\`, `\^`, `\$`, `\*`, `\+`, `\?`, `\{`, `\}`, `\[`, `\]`, `\(`, `\)`, and `\|`. Empty and `*` match all. It rejects `.`, non-ASCII patterns, negated/POSIX classes, flags/lookaround, other escapes, and misplaced anchors. Invalid regex on a retained group rejects config loading. The current Codex config test covers alternation/search matching, an ignored UserPromptSubmit matcher, and rejection of `Bash.*`; it does not exercise the full accepted grammar or the `*` match-all case. [Parser and regex subset](https://github.com/f4ah6o/dsh.mbt/blob/8683b3d076a453da802d80791745b36cfd9c8aa7/runtime/hooks_config.mbt#L90-L163), [Codex command/group parsing](https://github.com/f4ah6o/dsh.mbt/blob/8683b3d076a453da802d80791745b36cfd9c8aa7/runtime/hooks_config.mbt#L284-L376), [focused config test](https://github.com/f4ah6o/dsh.mbt/blob/8683b3d076a453da802d80791745b36cfd9c8aa7/runtime/hooks_wbtest.mbt#L2614-L2650).
- **CLI and runtime configuration:** hooks are opt-in through `--hooks-config PATH`; the candidate exposes `--hooks-dialect claude-code|codex` and Codex-only `--hooks-model NAME` for run, web, MCP, ACP, and desktop entry points. The dialect/model options require a config path and are accepted once; the runtime defaults the dialect to Claude Code and model to the static empty string. `NativeRuntime::open` and the desktop constructor accept the matching config/dialect/model options. The API's hook-context entry points carry `source_kind`, and its invocation-checkpoint method accepts `dialect`. [Option validation](https://github.com/f4ah6o/dsh.mbt/blob/8683b3d076a453da802d80791745b36cfd9c8aa7/runtime/cli_options.mbt#L303-L329), [usage](https://github.com/f4ah6o/dsh.mbt/blob/8683b3d076a453da802d80791745b36cfd9c8aa7/runtime/main.mbt#L98-L117), [runtime options](https://github.com/f4ah6o/dsh.mbt/blob/8683b3d076a453da802d80791745b36cfd9c8aa7/runtime/runtime.mbt#L128-L165), [desktop options](https://github.com/f4ah6o/dsh.mbt/blob/8683b3d076a453da802d80791745b36cfd9c8aa7/runtime/desktop.mbt#L15-L29), [API context options](https://github.com/f4ah6o/dsh.mbt/blob/8683b3d076a453da802d80791745b36cfd9c8aa7/api/api.mbt#L481-L556), [invocation dialect](https://github.com/f4ah6o/dsh.mbt/blob/8683b3d076a453da802d80791745b36cfd9c8aa7/api/api.mbt#L854-L872).
- **Payload and command behavior:** candidate builders emit Codex's snake-case payload without a trailing newline, use `transcript_path: null`, the configured static model, and `permission_mode: "default"`; UserPromptSubmit, tool, and Stop payload builders stringify the supplied turn ID. Tool input is reduced to `{ "command": <string-or-empty> }`; SessionStart has no `turn_id`, and Stop supplies `last_assistant_message: null` / `stop_hook_active: false`. The current root and child call sites do not pass the session's turn ID to the UserPromptSubmit, PreToolUse, PostToolUse, and Stop helpers; those helper defaults remain `0`. Wiring actual root/child turn IDs is an open follow-up, not covered by the payload-builder unit test that passes a fixture ID. The adapter runs handlers serially. Codex config has no per-hook environment map or plugin-root substitution; the child process uses a scrubbed `PATH` / locale environment, while `CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR` is injected only for Claude Code dialect. Exit 2 blocks using stderr; other nonzero exits pass. Structured stdout is parsed only on exit 0, and plain stdout is context only for SessionStart and UserPromptSubmit. The decoder can apply `block` / event-matched `permissionDecision: deny`; `allow` / `ask` do not grant permission or reopen an approval. It records `continue: false` diagnostically but does not stop the agent, and ignores `systemMessage` / `updatedInput` operationally. [Payload builders and turn-ID parameters](https://github.com/f4ah6o/dsh.mbt/blob/8683b3d076a453da802d80791745b36cfd9c8aa7/runtime/hooks_runtime.mbt#L172-L305), [defaulted event helpers](https://github.com/f4ah6o/dsh.mbt/blob/8683b3d076a453da802d80791745b36cfd9c8aa7/runtime/hooks_runtime.mbt#L647-L835), [Codex result decoder](https://github.com/f4ah6o/dsh.mbt/blob/8683b3d076a453da802d80791745b36cfd9c8aa7/runtime/hooks_codec.mbt#L383-L466), [child environment](https://github.com/f4ah6o/dsh.mbt/blob/8683b3d076a453da802d80791745b36cfd9c8aa7/runtime/hooks_process.mbt#L48-L65).
- **Activation and persisted provenance:** root SessionStart commands activate lazily while preparing the first valid root send; the candidate distinguishes newly created sessions from sessions present when the runtime opened. This is the native activation timing and should remain explicit beside the pinned handler/dispatch lifecycle recorded above; it is not by itself a demonstrated upstream defect. The candidate records `dialect` on hook-invoked events, marks admitted prompt-context messages with `source.kind = "hooks-codex"`, stores PostToolUse context provenance in `additional_contexts_source_kind`, and defaults old PostToolUse records lacking that field to `hooks-claude-code`. `moon.mod` remains at version `0.1.19` in this frozen commit; no minimum-reader/version bump is included, so compatibility and reader-floor policy remain unresolved before publication. [Root SessionStart activation](https://github.com/f4ah6o/dsh.mbt/blob/8683b3d076a453da802d80791745b36cfd9c8aa7/runtime/runtime.mbt#L784-L916), [invocation dialect](https://github.com/f4ah6o/dsh.mbt/blob/8683b3d076a453da802d80791745b36cfd9c8aa7/engine/hook_events.mbt#L154-L213), [prompt-context source kind](https://github.com/f4ah6o/dsh.mbt/blob/8683b3d076a453da802d80791745b36cfd9c8aa7/api/api.mbt#L481-L556), [PostToolUse restore default](https://github.com/f4ah6o/dsh.mbt/blob/8683b3d076a453da802d80791745b36cfd9c8aa7/engine/persistence.mbt#L1042-L1063).
- **Candidate-only verification and reproduced findings:** the implementation agent reports `moon check --target native` passing with four pre-existing vendor warnings, the runtime `Codex*` filter passing 4 / 4, the Engine `*hook*` filter passing 11 / 11, `moon fmt --check api engine runtime` passing, and `git diff --check` passing at exact `8683b3d`. Original output was not persisted, so these remain implementer-reported without logs. The four Codex-named runtime tests cover config, payload construction, output decoding, and diagnostic decisions; no real Codex subprocess integration or HTTP/MCP/ACP coverage is claimed. Sol’s independent native runtime probe contained 13 tests: 12 codec fixtures compared with a Node oracle importing the pinned TypeScript codec and merge modules, plus one accepted-regex differential test. The suite passed 9 / 13 and reproduced DEBT-045 / 046. The regex test compared 2,760 searches over 60 compiled cases (59 accepted non-sentinel patterns plus the empty-match sentinel) and 46 subjects with no mismatch; this is corpus evidence, not full JavaScript RegExp parity. The config boundary probe passed 2 / 3 and reproduced DEBT-049; the six-case Engine matrix passed 3 / 6 and reproduced DEBT-047 / 048, while a separate Engine boundary probe showed unsupported provenance accepted and then rejected by restore. The complete test set totals 23 tests, 14 passed / 9 failed. Sol’s exact-head review is Changes Requested (3 passes / 7 viewpoints; four major PORT findings and one minor trusted-API validation finding), not whole-feature approval. #### DEBT-045: Codex SessionStart structured context is dropped

- Origin / kind / priority / state: PORT / compatibility, Sol review severity major; priority unassessed, reproduced on frozen candidate `8683b3d076a453da802d80791745b36cfd9c8aa7`; fixed at the reproduced behavioral scope on exact head `425dafcecc9405009e7bdaca9ed98fc333489d94` (see retest checkpoint). This is a native adapter defect; it is not attributed to the pinned upstream runtime.
- Pinned source contract: the hook-protocol codec accepts event-matched `hookSpecificOutput.additionalContext` independently of the permission decision ([codec.ts](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/5badb15009ae1756c3afe0ae0cef1faafc290ccc/packages/hooks/hook-protocol/src/codec.ts#L112-L133)); the merge retains nonempty context ([merge.ts](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/5badb15009ae1756c3afe0ae0cef1faafc290ccc/packages/hooks/hook-protocol/src/merge.ts#L83-L85)). Codex SessionStart converts merged context into a user message and injects it into the created agent ([hooks-codex/src/index.ts](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/5badb15009ae1756c3afe0ae0cef1faafc290ccc/packages/hooks/hooks-codex/src/index.ts#L180-L200)). This is separate from DEBT-044's README lifecycle contradiction.
- Candidate path / observation: `native_hook_codex_result_decision` admits structured `additionalContext` only for PostToolUse or UserPromptSubmit and omits SessionStart ([hooks_codec.mbt](https://github.com/f4ah6o/dsh.mbt/blob/8683b3d076a453da802d80791745b36cfd9c8aa7/runtime/hooks_codec.mbt#L431-L454)). Sol's exact-candidate native fixture supplied exit 0 with `{"hookSpecificOutput":{"hookEventName":"SessionStart","additionalContext":"startup-note"}}`; the native decoder returned no context where the pinned codec/merge oracle returned `startup-note`. Reproducer command: `moon test runtime/reviewer_codex_foundation_wbtest.mbt --target native --verbose`. The fixture is test lines 29–35 of `/Volumes/devstorage/Developer/dsh-codex-command-hooks-builds-20261012/review-foundation-8683b3d/reviewer_codex_foundation_wbtest.mbt` (SHA-256 `16d7d2ebb8dafb0225eda261bd1b3da570a6a3e106172a82259b27c7cf7822f3`). The full 13-case runtime probe reports 9 passed / 4 failed; log `/Volumes/devstorage/Developer/dsh-codex-command-hooks-builds-20261012/review-foundation-8683b3d/runtime-independent-probes.log` (SHA-256 `15559d15163d39b917b514f86201abd1c527f5135a2b9731e3332922f619b8dd`). The JS oracle imports the actual pinned `codec.ts` and `merge.ts`; script `oracle.mjs` SHA-256 `5beacf9356a1bfb23d23db28f2614eed3eadcb0909009a08599e2f33e0125933`, result `oracle-results.json` SHA-256 `664b6906a850132bcc2fbe3f9e26d2dd329bb1545a909771373ab9e9161fd370`.
- Follow-up / acceptance: preserve the event-name check and allow valid SessionStart structured context to reach the SessionStart injection path while retaining the plain-stdout fallback behavior. The implementation agent committed correction `ceaff1b7e5f81f70a8607c59bfe2c6d505986102`; its earlier focused 1 / 1 test was run on base `8683b3d` plus patch SHA-256 `50a9bc3db192049ce1ceae4bde2b789151da3619b3f3620c71b442f5b7119543`, log `/Volumes/devstorage/Developer/dsh-codex-command-hooks-builds-20261012/test-codex-session-start-fix.log` (SHA-256 `a02b8bf047997e01cec210b67129510a0f04a182c014b4da91f739395976033a`), not as an exact-commit run. Sol later independently passed this behavior in the unchanged 23-test probe at exact `425dafc`; see the retest checkpoint.

#### DEBT-046: nested Codex permission-reason precedence differs from the source codec

- Origin / kind / priority / state: PORT / correctness, Sol review severity major; priority unassessed, reproduced on frozen candidate `8683b3d076a453da802d80791745b36cfd9c8aa7`; fixed at the reproduced behavioral scope on exact head `425dafcecc9405009e7bdaca9ed98fc333489d94` (see retest checkpoint). This is a native adapter defect; it is not an upstream bug claim.
- Pinned source contract: an event-matched valid `permissionDecision` overrides the top-level decision, while a string `permissionDecisionReason` independently overrides the top-level `reason`, including the empty string ([codec.ts](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/5badb15009ae1756c3afe0ae0cef1faafc290ccc/packages/hooks/hook-protocol/src/codec.ts#L105-L130)). The merged result uses the winning decision rank and includes only nonempty reasons at that rank ([merge.ts](https://github.com/deepseek-ai/deepseek-harness/blob/5badb15009ae1756c3afe0ae0cef1faafc290ccc/packages/hooks/hook-protocol/src/merge.ts#L71-L98)).
- Candidate path / observation: the decoder clears the top-level reason when a nested deny has no string reason, and only examines the nested reason inside the permission-decision branch; therefore a nested reason without a decision is ignored ([hooks_codec.mbt](https://github.com/f4ah6o/dsh.mbt/blob/8683b3d076a453da802d80791745b36cfd9c8aa7/runtime/hooks_codec.mbt#L420-L446)). Sol's exact-candidate native fixtures reproduced three distinct cases: (1) nested deny with no nested reason lost top-level `original objection`; (2) nested `specific objection` without a nested decision left top-level `old reason`; and (3) nested empty-string reason without a nested decision left `old reason` instead of overriding it. Reproducer command: `moon test runtime/reviewer_codex_foundation_wbtest.mbt --target native --verbose`. These are test lines 38–70 of the same probe source (SHA-256 above). Together with DEBT-045, the 13-case native run was 9 passed / 4 failed; the probe log, pinned-source oracle script, and result hashes are recorded under DEBT-045.
- Follow-up / acceptance: preserve the top-level reason if no nested reason string is present; apply a nested reason string independently of whether `permissionDecision` is present; and let the empty string override the top-level reason before the normal merge rules omit empty reasons. The implementation agent committed correction `e57732403db8f0e684f2075c81dee168564c2ee6`; its earlier focused 1 / 1 test used base `ceaff1b7e5f81f70a8607c59bfe2c6d505986102` plus patch SHA-256 `e0971dd819ca7b3d585f6a610a49d65f7fbe4918fcb1c7524fcc161285bc3961`, log `/Volumes/devstorage/Developer/dsh-codex-command-hooks-builds-20261012/test-codex-reason-fix.log` (SHA-256 `5f047e5126e2a05eb26feba6e99f893f645a45b19ba572c6cfbff6569eea4cb3`), not as an exact-commit run. Sol later independently passed these cases in the unchanged 23-test probe at exact `425dafc`; see the retest checkpoint.

#### DEBT-047: Codex Stop continuation snapshots cannot be restored

- Origin / kind / priority / state: PORT / correctness, persistence, Sol review severity major; priority unassessed, reproduced on frozen candidate `8683b3d076a453da802d80791745b36cfd9c8aa7`; fixed at the reproduced behavioral scope on exact head `425dafcecc9405009e7bdaca9ed98fc333489d94` (see retest checkpoint). The observed failures are in the native snapshot reader/writer; no upstream runtime defect is claimed.
- Candidate path / observation: the Stop continuation writer tags its staged reason message with `session.native_hook_context_source_kind` ([stop_hooks.mbt](https://github.com/f4ah6o/dsh.mbt/blob/8683b3d076a453da802d80791745b36cfd9c8aa7/engine/stop_hooks.mbt#L312-L317)), but the restore validator compares that first message against `hook_context_message([reason])`, whose default source is `hooks-claude-code` ([persistence.mbt](https://github.com/f4ah6o/dsh.mbt/blob/8683b3d076a453da802d80791745b36cfd9c8aa7/engine/persistence.mbt#L488-L494), [default source](https://github.com/f4ah6o/dsh.mbt/blob/8683b3d076a453da802d80791745b36cfd9c8aa7/engine/tool_calls.mbt#L16-L27)). Both root and child Codex Stop-continuation fixtures reached completed state but reopening their own snapshots returned `Err("Stop continuation message differs from its staged reason")` instead of `Ok(())`. Test source lines 27–60: `/Volumes/devstorage/Developer/dsh-codex-command-hooks-builds-20261012/review-foundation-8683b3d/reviewer_codex_engine_foundation_wbtest.mbt` (SHA-256 `0e384f5f126ca95d0aa50cc37aa8b3d547a26cc471bf87dfff4680478b4f3b6a`); exact-candidate six-case Engine log reports 3 passed / 3 failed at `/Volumes/devstorage/Developer/dsh-codex-command-hooks-builds-20261012/review-foundation-8683b3d/engine-independent-probes.log` (SHA-256 `fff5a729f93382dad57892e1a1924a467eea8c076230094d6482a36f99e1e349`), result JSON SHA-256 `4711dad4e126eb5dda36d8faec32d8fb7ac34086b709393579e6aae1473d8a28`.
- Follow-up / acceptance: restore must validate the committed Stop reason message against the same provenance kind used when written; keep root and child Codex snapshots reopenable and preserve Claude behavior and no-replay guarantees. The implementation agent committed correction `425dafcecc9405009e7bdaca9ed98fc333489d94`. Its earlier focused 1 / 1 Stop test was run on base `e57732403db8f0e684f2075c81dee168564c2ee6` plus patch SHA-256 `384687e823153d6f940e5262cb6fe963048ae49dc32a2ce0bdb4f76155471439`; the three-test log `/Volumes/devstorage/Developer/dsh-codex-command-hooks-builds-20261012/test-persistence-source-validation.log` (SHA-256 `9571e9479a12a4c511abf607bbb693e64949f2db81054f53578a3babc307556a`) is a base-plus-patch run, not an exact-commit run. Sol later independently passed both root and child restore cases at exact `425dafc`; see the retest checkpoint.

#### DEBT-048: native hook-context admission accepts unsupported provenance

- Origin / kind / priority / state: PORT / validation, persistence, Sol review severity minor; priority unassessed, reproduced on frozen candidate `8683b3d076a453da802d80791745b36cfd9c8aa7`; fixed at the reproduced behavioral scope on exact head `425dafcecc9405009e7bdaca9ed98fc333489d94` (see retest checkpoint). This concerns the trusted native admission boundary and its writer/reader contract for caller-supplied provenance; DEBT-047 concerns the built-in Codex Stop continuation message.
- Candidate path / observation: `Engine::apply_session_send_with_hook_contexts` validates context content but forwards `source_kind` without checking that it belongs to the source kinds the restore validator accepts ([transaction.mbt](https://github.com/f4ah6o/dsh.mbt/blob/8683b3d076a453da802d80791745b36cfd9c8aa7/engine/transaction.mbt#L386-L414)). The shared context-message builder writes the provided kind directly ([tool_calls.mbt](https://github.com/f4ah6o/dsh.mbt/blob/8683b3d076a453da802d80791745b36cfd9c8aa7/engine/tool_calls.mbt#L16-L27)); restore allows only `hooks-claude-code` and `hooks-codex` ([persistence.mbt](https://github.com/f4ah6o/dsh.mbt/blob/8683b3d076a453da802d80791745b36cfd9c8aa7/engine/persistence.mbt#L2135-L2148)). Sol’s six-case Engine matrix used `unsupported-source`, expected an atomic `Err`, and received a non-`Err` result. Reproducer command: `moon test engine/reviewer_codex_foundation_wbtest.mbt --target native --verbose`. A separate command, `moon test engine/reviewer_codex_boundary_wbtest.mbt --target native --verbose`, recorded `INVALID_SOURCE_ADMISSION=accepted` and `INVALID_SOURCE_RESTORE=Unsupported prompt context provenance`; its rejection assertion failed (0 / 1), demonstrating that an admitted snapshot cannot be restored. The boundary fixture `/Volumes/devstorage/Developer/dsh-codex-command-hooks-builds-20261012/review-foundation-8683b3d/reviewer_codex_engine_boundary_wbtest.mbt` has SHA-256 `f9a6a48d66cb6b55dbfb5c67072f7080b5a07260c4737dd2dade0b06fc4b4531`; log `/Volumes/devstorage/Developer/dsh-codex-command-hooks-builds-20261012/review-foundation-8683b3d/engine-boundary-probes.log` SHA-256 `749a0d1e66ec22d0ac532d15fc1767cd0378de49844af56a4ddfd6715d2a3efa`, result JSON SHA-256 `36172f95f004ba0bd2ecfe4d773683e8b2c03ddb67b4d6169f2de45730573438`. The six-case source/log and 3 / 6 result are listed under DEBT-047. This is a narrower trusted direct/native API boundary: the ordinary remote JSON schema is closed and active-dialect callers pass supported constants; no remote-input vulnerability is claimed.
- Follow-up / acceptance: reject any unsupported source kind before mutation, or share validation so every accepted prompt-context record is readable after reopen. The implementation agent committed atomic rejection in `425dafcecc9405009e7bdaca9ed98fc333489d94`; its earlier focused 1 / 1 test used base `e57732403db8f0e684f2075c81dee168564c2ee6` plus patch SHA-256 `384687e823153d6f940e5262cb6fe963048ae49dc32a2ce0bdb4f76155471439`, in the base-plus-patch log cited under DEBT-047. Sol later independently verified rejection and unchanged behavior at exact `425dafc`; see the retest checkpoint.

#### DEBT-049: Codex regex support regresses hyphenated Claude subagent matchers

- Origin / kind / priority / state: PORT / compatibility, Sol review severity major; priority unassessed, reproduced on frozen candidate `8683b3d076a453da802d80791745b36cfd9c8aa7`; fixed at the reproduced behavioral scope on exact head `425dafcecc9405009e7bdaca9ed98fc333489d94` (see retest checkpoint). This is a regression in the existing Claude Code dialect introduced by the matcher change, not an upstream defect.
- Main / candidate comparison: main `69c51a543b40ca909db8f1f888ec5065804a75ea` allowed `-` in `native_hook_valid_literal_matcher` ([main source](https://github.com/f4ah6o/dsh.mbt/blob/69c51a543b40ca909db8f1f888ec5065804a75ea/runtime/hooks_config.mbt#L67-L85)); the frozen Codex candidate removed that character from the same validator ([candidate source](https://github.com/f4ah6o/dsh.mbt/blob/8683b3d076a453da802d80791745b36cfd9c8aa7/runtime/hooks_config.mbt#L70-L87)). Since `native_hook_parse_config` defaults to Claude Code, this rejects the previously accepted `general-purpose` matcher for SubagentStart and SubagentStop.
- Observation / reproducer / result: Sol’s exact-candidate native test configures both subagent events with matcher `general-purpose`; parsing now returns `hook matcher must use literal tool names separated by |; regex is unsupported` instead of retaining both groups. Reproducer command: `moon test runtime/reviewer_codex_boundary_wbtest.mbt --target native --verbose`. Test source lines 2–10 of `/Volumes/devstorage/Developer/dsh-codex-command-hooks-builds-20261012/review-foundation-8683b3d/reviewer_codex_boundary_wbtest.mbt` (SHA-256 `972a9a997add64a467cea9c7f98ec4f96e6beadc5b45f15c432cd629e43cda67`). This was also the shipped `general-purpose` example and documented literal subject ([native-runtime.md](https://github.com/f4ah6o/dsh.mbt/blob/8683b3d076a453da802d80791745b36cfd9c8aa7/docs/native-runtime.md#L605-L613), [subagent contract](https://github.com/f4ah6o/dsh.mbt/blob/8683b3d076a453da802d80791745b36cfd9c8aa7/docs/native-runtime.md#L744-L747)). The native config boundary run passed 2 / 3 and failed this regression; log `/Volumes/devstorage/Developer/dsh-codex-command-hooks-builds-20261012/review-foundation-8683b3d/runtime-boundary-probes.log` (SHA-256 `84299870ea65112f1694ec4c3fef951a7fa9a56bcf0b3f411fc1ca0b4ceb2eca`), result JSON SHA-256 `4de92a161247dd78cc5ed1f3910ba0bf7cd47beb887096d1ae3938807c1902ec`.
- Follow-up / acceptance: preserve the baseline Claude matcher alphabet while keeping Codex regex parsing isolated by dialect. The implementation agent restored `-` in `425dafcecc9405009e7bdaca9ed98fc333489d94`; its earlier focused 1 / 1 compatibility test used base `e57732403db8f0e684f2075c81dee168564c2ee6` plus patch SHA-256 `384687e823153d6f940e5262cb6fe963048ae49dc32a2ce0bdb4f76155471439`, in the base-plus-patch log cited under DEBT-047. Sol later independently passed the unchanged matcher fixture at exact `425dafc`; see the retest checkpoint.

### Codex hook foundation — exact 425dafc behavioral retest

Sol reran the four unchanged private probe sources from the failed-head review in a fresh snapshot at exact candidate `425dafcecc9405009e7bdaca9ed98fc333489d94`. The exact command was `moon test runtime/reviewer_codex_foundation_wbtest.mbt runtime/reviewer_codex_boundary_wbtest.mbt engine/reviewer_codex_foundation_wbtest.mbt engine/reviewer_codex_boundary_wbtest.mbt --target native --verbose`; it passed 23 / 23 (result JSON records this exact head and exit 0). This independently verifies all five DEBT-045–049 behaviors at their reproduced scope: structured SessionStart context; nested reason precedence; completed root and child Stop snapshot restore; unsupported source rejection before mutation; and Claude `general-purpose` matcher compatibility. The probe did not broaden runtime integration or establish whole-feature acceptance.

The same 13-test runtime probe contains 12 codec fixtures and one regex differential test. The regex differential matched 2,760 searches across 60 compiled cases (59 accepted non-sentinel patterns and the empty-match sentinel) against 46 subjects. It provides evidence for that corpus only, not full JavaScript RegExp parity. The four original probe-source hashes are preserved in `/Volumes/devstorage/Developer/dsh-codex-command-hooks-builds-20261012/review-fixed-425dafc/unchanged-probe-sources.json` (SHA-256 `bf8659f9bd8c735e2ff1e5e71edd59ab34268da5a8b91b593e01847c44efe25e`); the exact-head source snapshot archive is `exact-fixed-425dafc-source.tar` (SHA-256 `2338e9b48c8b4005d30f961faabed4275fac245bfdb27cf36889a2d7655a5f7b`). Combined probe log `/Volumes/devstorage/Developer/dsh-codex-command-hooks-builds-20261012/review-fixed-425dafc/all-independent-probes.log` SHA-256 `9293c7f780adfd6414f27ff341a5cb283d37408a6e4d945f0272c6a1f58a9905`; result JSON `all-independent-probes-result.json` SHA-256 `9ac7488f870076a3d9bc7c58ea73e096d3660cb2b55a4eeb74faca24d67c6adc`.

At the same exact head, the repository Codex filter `moon test runtime/hooks_wbtest.mbt --target native --filter "Codex*" --verbose` passed 3 / 4. Its lone failure is the stale assertion at `runtime/hooks_wbtest.mbt:2650` that `native_hook_valid_literal_matcher("Bash-Tool")` is false; restoring the pre-existing hyphen support makes that assertion obsolete. Log `/Volumes/devstorage/Developer/dsh-codex-command-hooks-builds-20261012/review-fixed-425dafc/existing-codex-tests.log` SHA-256 `3a83ed83a406fc28012934662b983863f44bd593fc0f0d29b6b81e1ff9f58de6`; result JSON SHA-256 `2994eb97f4fe90ccea0ffd60813f58e1a5ff5c0f45e91facac48c56dd0d54079`. Luna is updating this existing test in an uncommitted integration follow-up. The base-plus-patch focused logs for `ceaff1b`, `e577324`, and `425dafc` are explicitly attributed on DEBT-045–049; only this Sol probe is the independent exact-commit retest. Full runtime/Engine suite coverage, end-to-end turn-ID wiring, whole-feature approval, CI, merge, and release remain pending.

#### DEBT-050: Provider adapters reject Codex hook-context messages

- Origin / kind / priority / state: PORT / provider integration correctness, Sol severity major; priority unassessed; independently reproduced on exact candidate `e7ef4746323a7340b52a62925e90b3211635ed00`. The failure is in the native provider source-kind gate, not an upstream DeepSeek Harness runtime bug. Provider correction `4ce2790d840a9abb720ef1d1b01504e3b94f7d7a` is committed; Sol's exact fixed-head retest remains pending.
- Candidate path / observation: Codex hook invocation sets prompt-context provenance to `hooks-codex` ([hook event source](https://github.com/f4ah6o/dsh.mbt/blob/e7ef4746323a7340b52a62925e90b3211635ed00/engine/hook_events.mbt#L199-L204)). The shared provider helper accepts only the single-key source object `{"kind":"hooks-claude-code"}` ([source-kind gate](https://github.com/f4ah6o/dsh.mbt/blob/e7ef4746323a7340b52a62925e90b3211635ed00/provider/json.mbt#L84-L91)); both OpenAI/DeepSeek preparation and Responses request construction reject content-block arrays unless this predicate succeeds ([prepare path](https://github.com/f4ah6o/dsh.mbt/blob/e7ef4746323a7340b52a62925e90b3211635ed00/provider/prepare.mbt#L96-L102), [Responses path](https://github.com/f4ah6o/dsh.mbt/blob/e7ef4746323a7340b52a62925e90b3211635ed00/provider/responses.mbt#L43-L49)). Therefore otherwise valid `hooks-codex` user context becomes `INVALID_REQUEST` on all three routes.
- Exact-head reproduction: Sol’s independent native provider matrix used five valid hook-context histories (root SessionStart, root UserPromptSubmit, child UserPromptSubmit, correlated PostToolUse, and Stop continuation) across OpenAI Chat Completions, DeepSeek Messages, and OpenAI Responses: all 15 valid Codex cases failed with `INVALID_REQUEST`; all 18 malformed-provenance cases were rejected as expected. The six invalid variants were unknown kind, extra source field, numeric kind, missing kind, string source, and null source. The public-serializer test command was `moon test provider/reviewer_codex_provenance_test.mbt --target native --verbose --target-dir /Volumes/devstorage/Developer/dsh-codex-command-hooks-builds-20261012/review-integration-e7ef474/provider-test-build`, at source head `e7ef474...`; result: 18 passed / 15 failed. Probe source `/Volumes/devstorage/Developer/dsh-codex-command-hooks-builds-20261012/review-integration-e7ef474/reviewer_codex_provenance_test.mbt` SHA-256 `03e82714c1b7013e749a9823bf4b264df9d91f2d02a55ba72743aff1b7d8cd9f`; log `provider-independent-probes.log` SHA-256 `64b316df5d33c5c5802679edceb191862401d147fbf091e8e119969c9b982e99`; result JSON SHA-256 `4a0e0e5eec4ef139e28bb59e5d94cefae1a756537564e8e49b39f601d3dde594`. Sol’s exact-head report is Changes Requested, scope-limited to this major PORT defect (3 passes / 7 viewpoints): `/Volumes/devstorage/Developer/dsh-codex-command-hooks-builds-20261012/review-integration-e7ef474/review-integration-e7ef474-report.md`, SHA-256 `cd18ab2bf3ba74796203aad1890d56112c846f61cfe1a7032d4e951bd263c3a1`. Exact-e7 worker identity is in `fresh-worker-identity.json` SHA-256 `c2a5d575dc3c73c96cca0b9eb8b0cd1be17f3ebd1a06d5833baeac76eb6b57cb`; worker SHA-256 `71a83dadd6aef95a99822a52e004e280fb9719972458aae98b3b8b709805176c`. This matrix compares public serializers, not live HTTP dispatch on all three routes.
- Real-worker confirmation: Luna’s root HTTP/reopen/fork probe at source commit `e7ef474...` plus test-only tracked patch SHA-256 `6df198b3ef8aa1131c11eadf17f42325a884a13076e1263be1e69db0375a8de9` reached the actual provider request. The event trace contains the emitted user message with `source.kind = "hooks-codex"`, followed by `INVALID_REQUEST: content blocks are supported only for hooks-claude-code user messages`; this is the product failure, not a runner launch failure. Worker SHA-256 `19504104bcc6d6853e52e98d444930d89b6e0387769393fd9b19ec55ce35e8db`; failure log `/Volumes/devstorage/Developer/dsh-codex-command-hooks-builds-20261012/test-codex-root-http-failure-detail.log` SHA-256 `d61d32ebf4bca5b99c15a3d4b82c7f1f187b0b3c6aac868290a77ab12603f1b6`.
- Harness distinctions: an earlier attempt to invoke the generated test executable as `--internal-worker` aborted in `internal_parse_args` (`test-codex-root-http-runner-probe-2.log`, SHA-256 `51ccf61534b668d6b4d6788fb590ff2c88c9f2b56212219ff445fe35c09c57a2`); that setup failure is not a provider defect. Sol’s broader three-test e7 runtime integration attempt timed out after 180 seconds waiting for requests the provider gate rejected before they reached the fixture server; no suite count is claimed. An earlier controlled-job rejection came from a fixture calling `run_provider_effect` directly; the current root fixture uses `run_effects` and passes a `NativeJobControl`, so the earlier mismatch is not a product regression. The root fixture also accumulates two prompt contexts and two Stop diagnostics (pre-turn prompt diagnostics intentionally have no paired message); the child fixture manually completes the parent follow-up and therefore expects only the child Stop. These fixture corrections are not additional product findings.
